# rq6-candidates-dev

RQ6, the final-model candidates against the opponent on development data, three seeds
each, all trained on MetaCentrum (fobos, NVIDIA L40S): GLiNER focal loss with the real
corpora 3x (train-cs-sk-focal-r3), GLiNER cross-entropy and focal loss with WikiGoldSK
and SlavicNER added (train-cs-sk-ce-more, train-cs-sk-focal-more), the XLM-R token
classifier with them (train-cs-sk-xlmr-ce-more), and the union of XLM-R and GLiNER
focal with the added corpora, seed paired with seed, since XLM-R leads on CNEC and
UNER-SK and GLiNER focal on REDACT. Against NameTag 3 multilingual (the headline
opponent), NameTag 3 Czech and zero-shot GLiNER, on the dev splits of real text and
WikiGoldSK dev. Train train-cs-sk-focal-more first.
